Now I'll use the `show_transactions` API with these dates to get all transactions for the current month. I'll also set `page_index` to 0 and `page_limit` to a higher value to ensure I get all pages.\n\nCode:\n```python\nvenmo_access_token = login_result[\"access_token\"]\ntransactions = []\npage_index = 0\npage_limit = 100  # Increase page limit to ensure all transactions are retrieved\n\nwhile True:\n    transaction_page = apis.venmo.show_transactions(\n        access_token=<REDACTED:CREDENTIAL:SHA256=85b27df69f7f86d046a12ee242184f78a6a7226560320b388801c459cf22c463>,\n        min_created_at=min_created_at,\n        max_created_at=max_created_at,\n        page_index=page_index,\n        page_limit=page_limit\n    )\n    if not transaction_page:\n        break\n    transactions.extend(transaction_page)\n    page_index += 1\n\nprint(f\"Found {len(transactions)} transactions for the current month\")\n```",

      "content": "I can see that the `show_transactions` API allows filtering by `min_created_at` and `max_created_at` to get transactions for the current month. I need to calculate the current date and set `min_created_at` to the start of the current month and `max_created_at` to the end of the current month. Let me first get the current date and calculate the start and end of the current month.\n\nCode:\n```python\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta\n\n# Get current date\ncurrent_date = datetime.now()\n\n# Calculate start of the current month\nstart_of_month = current_date.replace(day=1)\n\n# Calculate end of the current month\nif current_date.month == 12:\n    end_of_month = current_date.replace(year=current_date.year + 1, month=1, day=1) - timedelta(days=1)\nelse:\n    end_of_month = current_date.replace(month=current_date.month + 1, day=1) - timedelta(days=1)\n\n# Format dates in YYYY-MM-DD format\nmin_created_at = start_of_month.strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d\")\nmax_created_at = end_of_month.strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d\")\n\nprint(f\"min_created_at: {min_created_at}, max_created_at: {max_created_at}\")\n```",
